A benchmark consisting of twenty real-world traffic rules was used to evaluate each approach in terms of syntactic validity, semantic correctness, and logical completeness. $S4L\rightarrow Prolog$ achieves the highest accuracy, correctly formalizing 75 percent of the rules, while $NL\rightarrow Prolog$ reaches 60 percent and $LE\rightarrow Prolog$ reaches 55 percent. Qualitative analysis further shows that S4L captures implicit causal relations, deontic modality, and exception structure more reliably than the baselines. These results demonstrate that structured reasoning prompts can substantially improve the reliability of natural-language-to-logic translation for legal and safety-critical applications.

</details>


### [176] [A Qualitative Model for Reasoning about Path and Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20349)
*Abhishek Jaiswal,Zoe Falomir*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个混合定性推理模型，用于Camelot Jr.积木拼图游戏，旨在通过游戏训练儿童空间推理能力，并实现可解释的玩家指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间推理能力与STEM领域表现密切相关，游戏是训练儿童空间技能的有效媒介，但需要AI agent具备常识推理能力以提供类似人类导师的指导。定性推理(QR)模型适合处理这类需要符号表示和可解释性的应用场景。

Method: 提出一个混合定性模型，整合数学质心稳定性逻辑来处理游戏所需的精确物理推理，用于指导定性求解器。游戏要求玩家建造多层桥梁连接两个塔上的角色，需要使平台稳定、规划路径并使用所有积木。

Result: 该模型能够将游戏状态转换为可解释的反馈，实现类似人类导师的玩家指导。

Conclusion: 本工作促进了Camelot Jr.游戏中的空间技能训练，为开发以人为中心、可解释的游戏Playing代理做出贡献。

Abstract: Spatial reasoning abilities correlate strongly with performance in STEM fields. Games offer a compelling medium for training these critical skills in developing children who have a natural proclivity for play. However, to facilitate human-like tutoring and player guidance, these games require an AI agent capable of making commonsense inferences from spatial events. Qualitative reasoning (QR) models appear to be a suitable framework for these application domains. As these models reason in symbolic representations, they can seamlessly translate game states into interpretable feedback for human-like player guidance. This paper introduces a hybrid qualitative model designed for Camelot Jr., a block-puzzle game that requires constructing multi-level bridges to connect two avatars stationed on separate towers. The game poses a challenge for the player, who must make platforms stable, plan their path, and ensure they use all the provided blocks. To handle the precise physics required by the domain, we integrate a mathematical center-of-mass stability logic to guide our qualitative solver. Our work facilitates spatial skill training in Camelot Jr. and contributes to the development of human-centric, explainable game-playing agents.

</details>


### [177] [Generating Heterogeneous 3D Geological Microstructures from 2D Images via a Stable Diffusion-Adversarial Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20358)
*Ali Aouf,Eric Laloy,Bart Rogiers,Christophe De Vleeschouwer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合方法，结合去噪扩散模型的稳定性和生成质量，从2D图像重建3D微观结构，解决了传统GAN方法（如SliceGAN）在处理复杂非均质微观结构时细节捕捉不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D成像成本高、可及性差且技术受限，需要从更易获取的2D图像重建3D体积。现有GAN方法SliceGAN在处理复杂非均质微观结构时难以捕捉精细细节，需要改进的生成框架。

Method: 提出混合方法，使用去噪扩散模型，由于无3Dground truth，用对抗损失替代标准去噪损失，实现稳定训练。

Result: 模型能生成不同复杂度的微观结构，切片伪影最小，与ground truth相分数和结构描述符高度一致。

Conclusion: 该混合方法成功结合了去噪扩散模型的稳定性和生成质量，为复杂材料的3D微观结构重建提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Characterizing the physical properties of clay and cementitious materials matters across many fields, from materials science to geological waste disposal. Property simulation typically calls for 3D imaging, which is expensive, not always accessible, and technically limited for certain materials. Recent progress in deep generative models offers a way around this, reconstructing 3D volumes from the more easily acquired 2D images.
  Among GAN-based methods for 3D microstructure generation, SliceGAN has shown strong results for homogeneous isotropic and anisotropic systems. It struggles, however, to capture the finer detail of more complex heterogeneous microstructures, which motivates alternative generative frameworks.
  We introduce a hybrid approach that draws on the stability and generation quality of denoising diffusion models. Since no 3D ground truth is available, we replace the standard denoising loss with an adversarial loss, which yields a stable training process in our experiments. We show that the resulting model generates microstructures of varying complexity with minimal slice artefacts and close agreement with ground-truth phase fractions and structural descriptors.

</details>


### [178] [The Organization of Inference: Information, Resource Constraints, and AI Production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20449)
*Yukun Zhang,Kemu Xu,Yishen Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究AI生产中推理的经济价值，分析能力与任务信息在生产各阶段的分布如何影响组织边界。通过受控工作流实验验证软件工程任务，直接执行与信息约束规划的成功率差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解AI生产中不同组织形式的经济价值，特别是直接执行与规划工作流的比较

Method: 在受控工作流实验中使用外部验证的软件工程任务，比较直接执行与规划两种方式在不同token限制下的表现

Result: 直接执行在12000和24000token时成功率均为59.6%；规划从36.2%升至51.2%。信息约束规划劣势缩小15个百分点。任务知情规划在24000token时比直接执行高29.6个百分点。

Conclusion: 规模决定系统可用能力；工作流和信息结构塑造生产价值。规划工作流在更高token容量下更具优势。

Abstract: The economic value of inference depends on how capacity and task information are distributed across stages of AI production. We study these organizational margins using controlled workflow experiments on externally verified software-engineering tasks. In two matched resource panels, direct execution records the same success rate of 59.6 percent at logical-token ceilings of 12,000 and 24,000, while success under information-constrained planning rises from 36.2 to 51.2 percent. The planning disadvantage narrows by 15.0 percentage points (95 percent task-cluster bootstrap interval: 4.2 to 25.8). A strict read-only planning campaign varies whether the planner sees the task issue. At 12,000 tokens, issue access raises success by about 16 percentage points over issue-hidden planning. Compared with direct execution, task-informed planning is about 10 points lower at 12,000 tokens; at 24,000 tokens, it shows a 29.6-point advantage. In the resource panels, direct execution uses substantially less than either ceiling, while the planning workflow's binding rate falls from 46.2 to 0.8 percent and downstream execution accounts for 89.9 percent of the increase in total use. Scale determines the capacity available to a system; workflow and information structure shape the productive value

</details>


### [179] [SkillAA: Attribution-Guided Skill-Graph Updating with Targeted Validation and Rollback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20455)
*Ziqiao Shang,Ling-Yue Ge,Lan-Zhe Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SkillAA是一个针对冻结语言模型的结构化技能优化框架,通过统一的图结构表示技能适用性、执行和组合,支持技能选择、归因引导修复和更新验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法存在两个问题:1)直接从失败 rollout 编辑技能,缺乏从观察到失败到可编辑位置的结构化路由;2)技能图谱未充分利用语义边界、对象地址和拓扑依赖进行技能检索、目标更新和作用域验证。

Method: SkillAA对比成功和失败执行,将候选修复路由到特定图对象,仅更新选定的局部结构,并使用Local和Big Gates在提交前筛选候选变更。框架统一表示技能适用性、执行和组合,同一结构支持技能选择、归因引导修复和更新验证。

Result: 在gpt-5.6-sol上,SkillAA在SearchQA、LiveMath和DocVQA上分别达到81.5%、66.7%和91.2%的准确率,在每个主要设置中都达到了最高观察均值。

Conclusion: 归因引导图编辑和图作用域验证具有实用价值,该框架成功地将技能优化过程结构化,为冻结语言模型的技能编辑提供了有效方法。

Abstract: External skills provide domain procedures without parameter updates, but existing methods often edit skills directly from failed rollouts without structured routing from an observed failure to an editable location; existing skill graphs also underuse semantic boundaries, object addresses, and topological dependencies for skill retrieval, targeted updating, and scoped validation. We introduce SkillAA (Skill Abductive Attribution), a structured skill-optimization framework for frozen language models. It represents skill applicability, execution, and composition in a unified graph, allowing the same structure to support skill selection, attribution-guided repair, and update validation. SkillAA contrasts successful and failed executions to route candidate repairs to specific graph objects, updates only the selected local structure, and uses Local and Big Gates to screen candidate changes before commitment. With gpt-5.6-sol, SkillAA reaches 81.5%, 66.7%, and 91.2% on SearchQA, LiveMath, and DocVQA, respectively, and attains the highest observed mean in every main setting. These results support the utility of attribution-guided graph editing and graph-scoped validation.

</details>


### [180] [How Do Agent Harnesses Create Value? Planning Information and Release Control in Stateful LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20474)
*Yukun Zhang,Kemu Xu,Yishen Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究AI代理中规划指导与验证器的作用，通过零售和航空实验发现：预写计划相比伪计划提升7.17%成功率，验证器可拒绝61%的错误案例但会遗漏17%正确案例。哪个组件更重要取决于错误接受 liability：低liability时规划增益主导，高liability时验证器避免错误通过的优势主导，单独验证器能以较低成本获得接近完整的收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解AI代理系统中规划指导（planning guidance）、执行组织（execution organization）和完成检查（completion checking）三个组件对任务成功、错误接受和成本的影响，以优化代理设计。

Method: 在τ²-bench上开展两个零售实验和一个航空试点，比较预写任务特定计划（Fixed）与匹配词数的打乱策略文本（Sham），使用只读终端验证器和神谕验证器评估265个匹配单元。

Result: Fixed提升神谕验证成功7.17个百分点（90%置信区间1.15-13.36），收益集中于高复杂度任务。验证器拒绝61%的无效案例但遗漏17%正确案例，每案例增加成本不到1美分。

Conclusion: 组件重要性取决于错误接受的损失值：低liability时规划增益主导，高liability时验证器避免错误通过的优势主导，单独验证器能以较低成本获得接近完整的收益。设计代理系统时需根据应用场景的liability选择合适组件组合。

Abstract: Agent harnesses supply planning guidance, organize execution, and check completion. We study how these components affect success, erroneous acceptance, and cost in two Retail experiments and an Airline pilot in $τ^2$-bench. The primary comparison pairs prewritten task-specific plans (Fixed) with shuffled policy text matched in word count (Sham), isolating the contribution of guidance content. Across 265 matched cells, Fixed improves oracle-verified success by 7.17 percentage points (90\% task-clustered bootstrap interval, 1.15--13.36 points), with gains concentrated in higher-complexity tasks. A read-only terminal verifier rejects 61\% of Retail oracle-invalid episodes while withholding 17\% of correct ones, at less than one cent of additional cost per episode. Which component matters more depends on the loss assigned to erroneous acceptance: at low liability the planning gain dominates; at high liability the verifier's avoided false passes dominate---and a standalone verifier captures nearly all the false-pass benefit of the full planning-plus-verification stack at a fraction of its cost.

</details>


### [181] [SoL-Pi: Recursively Scaling Auto-Research Loops for Efficient Agent Harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20519)
*Haozhe Liu,Tian Ye,Sensen Gao,Qihang Cao,Yitong Li,Mingchen Zhuge,Duomin Wang,Ruihua Zhang,Ping Luo,Jiawang Bian,Lei Zhu,Ligeng Zhu,Enze Xie,Song Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SoL-Pi系统，通过四个机制（动作执行、上下文压缩、观察处理、委托阅读）实现token效率提升，在EdgeBench上比Pi节省44.7-49.0%的token流量和约1/3的API成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着编码智能体从监督式代码补全转向无人值守的持续探索，任务从孤立的预测扩展到长推理轨迹，token效率对扩展递归自我改进变得重要。

Method: 采用RSI启发的方法，在harness层扩展自动研究循环。四个机制通过选择存活：动作执行、上下文压缩、观察处理、委托阅读，形成SoL-Pi系统。

Result: 在51任务的EdgeBench上，SoL-Pi性能与Pi相当，但token流量减少44.7-49.0%，API成本降低约1/3。每小时节省$8.75-$13.50（相比原生Codex和Claude Code harnesses），$4.36-$5.71（相比Pi）。

Conclusion: SoL-Pi实现了token效率的显著提升，在保持性能的同时大幅降低成本，为生产级自动harness发现奠定了基础。

Abstract: As coding agents move from supervised code completion to unattended, around-the-clock exploration, their work expands from isolated predictions into long trajectories of reasoning, tool use, and feedback. Token efficiency therefore becomes important for scaling recursive self-improvement. We take an RSI-inspired approach at the harness layer, scaling auto-research loops across increasingly numerous and diverse environments for harness rollouts. At this scale, the process yields reusable improvements that transfer beyond their development setting, moving automated harness discovery toward production-level outcomes. Four mechanisms survive selection and form SoL-Pi, spanning action execution, context compaction, observation handling, and delegated reading. On the 51-task EdgeBench evaluation, SoL-Pi achieves performance comparable to Pi across GPT-5.6 Sol and Opus 5 while reducing recorded token traffic by 44.7-49.0% and API cost by about one third. In other words, estimated hourly savings are \$8.75-\$13.50 relative to native Codex and Claude Code harnesses, and \$4.36-\$5.71 relative to Pi.

</details>


### [182] [FreqCondNorm: Towards Cross-domain Predictive Maintenance through a Frequency-Conditioned Transformer Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20535)
*Zaynab Raounak,Camille LHermine,Zhiguo Zeng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FreqCondNorm是一种基于Transformer的深度学习模型，通过引入FiLM风格的频率条件归一化层来解决跨机器和操作条件的迁移问题。该模型在5个公开预测维护数据集上进行预训练，在故障诊断任务上展现出优异的迁移性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习预测维护模型在不同机器和操作条件间迁移能力差，尤其是在标记数据稀缺且信号采样频率跨越五个数量级（1 Hz至100 kHz）的情况下问题更为突出。

Method: 提出FreqCondNorm架构：1) 使用Transformer作为基础架构；2) 引入FiLM风格的频率条件归一化层来统一异构时间序列；3) 采用掩码自编码和对比学习进行预训练，并使用平衡领域采样；4) 在5个公共数据集（CWRU, MFPT, UOC18, PRONOSTIA, CMAPSS）上训练。

Result: 故障诊断任务：1) CWRU数据集准确率达99.2%，比CNN提升6.4个百分点；2) MFPT数据集零样本准确率达82.1%，展现出跨采样频率的强大迁移能力。但该方法在剩余使用寿命（RUL）预测上未能提升性能。

Conclusion: FreqCondNorm在故障诊断的跨域迁移方面取得显著成效，但预训练目标与RUL预测目标存在不匹配，需进一步研究改进。

Abstract: Deep learning predictive maintenance models suffer from poor transferability across machines and operating conditions, especially when labelled data are scarce and signals span five orders of magnitude in sampling frequency (1 Hz to ~100 kHz). We propose FreqCondNorm, a Transformer-based architecture that introduces a FiLM-style frequency-conditioned normalization layer to unify heterogeneous time-series within a single model. The architecture is pretrained on five public predictive maintenance datasets (CWRU, MFPT, UOC18, PRONOSTIA, CMAPSS) using masked auto-encoding and contrastive learning with balanced domain sampling. On fault diagnosis, the model achieves 99.2% accuracy on CWRU (+6.4 pp over CNN) and 82.1% zero-shot accuracy on MFPT, demonstrating strong transfer across sampling frequencies. However, the approach does not improve remaining useful life prediction, suggesting a mismatch between pretraining and RUL objectives that warrants future investigation.

</details>


### [183] [Refuse, Decompose, Refresh: A Claim-Safe Protocol for Closed-Loop AI Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20538)
*Peiying Zhu,Sidi Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对闭合环系统的AI评估协议，旨在解决评估可重复但支持错误结论的风险。该协议包含三个行动：拒绝（无干净参考流时放弃）、分解（分别报告协议执行、运行误入和结构假设）、刷新（将分布偏移警报视为重新计算参考图的请求）。在包含24个策略组件、三个需求机制的模拟器中验证了该协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭合环系统中策略决定访问状态、可观察组件和哪些故障留下可测量痕迹，导致AI评估虽可完美重现但仍可能支持错误结论。这一问题在政策影响的系统中尤为严重。

Method: 提出claim-safe协议，包含三个核心行动：(1)拒绝：当缺乏干净参考流或匹配的运行时比较时放弃；(2)分解：分别报告协议执行、操作误入和结构假设，而非单一PASS/FAIL标签；(3)刷新：将分布偏移警报视为重新计算参考图的请求，而非故障证据。在24策略组件、三需求机制、双故障掩码族、独立开发种子的聚合模拟器中实例化验证。

Result: 预注册测试集包含1440个案例和21600个分区行。结果：仅55/72个机制组件单元被参考接受，54/55保持运行时接受；稳定误入为0/20表示组件，单侧精确95%上界为0.1391；在接受单元内，受影响干净流量预测优于标称故障单元分数，540单元行（嵌套于20组件集群）中单元减去流量的负对数似然差为0.1264nats/行，95%组件集群区间为[0.0593, 0.1918]。漂移日志显示为何"空"必须参考相关：干净故障空流在三个机制中触发15/15、0/15和14/15警报，只有中间机制匹配冻结检测器参考。

Conclusion: 贡献了一个可执行契约，将可观察支持、统计校准和合理主张联系起来，而非通用阈值。该协议通过拒绝、分解、刷新三动作确保AI评估结果与有效主张相匹配，避免因分布偏移导致的错误结论。

Abstract: An AI evaluation can be perfectly reproducible and still support the wrong claim. This risk is acute in closed-loop systems: policy determines visited states, observable components, and which failures leave a measurable trace. We propose a claim-safe protocol with three actions. Refuse: abstain when a clean reference stream or matched runtime comparison lacks support. Decompose: report protocol execution, operational false admission, and structural hypotheses separately rather than as one PASS/FAIL label. Refresh: treat distribution-shift alarms as requests to invalidate and recompute a reference map, not as fault evidence. We instantiate the protocol in an aggregate-only simulator with 24 policy components, three demand regimes, two fault-mask families, and independent development and heldout seeds. The preregistered heldout contains 1,440 cases and 21,600 partition rows. Only 55/72 regime-component units were reference-admitted and 54/55 remained runtime-admitted, making abstention part of the result. Stable false admission was 0/20 represented components, with a one-sided exact 95% upper bound of 0.1391 under a frozen 0.20 rule. Within admitted units, affected clean traffic outpredicted nominal fault-cell fraction: across 540 unit-arm rows nested in 20 component clusters, the cell-minus-traffic negative-log-likelihood difference was 0.1264 nats per row, with a 95% component-cluster interval of [0.0593, 0.1918]. A drift log shows why "null" must be reference-relative: clean fault-null streams triggered 15/15, 0/15, and 14/15 alarms across three regimes, while only the middle regime matched the frozen detector reference. Rather than a universal threshold, we contribute an executable contract linking observable support, statistical calibration, and justified claims.

</details>


### [184] [Limits of Confidence in Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20581)
*Russ Webb,Amitis Shidani,Alice Bizeul,Dan Busbridge*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了离散扩散模型（特别是remasking和uniform-state samplers）在多位置写入时无法正确建模token之间依赖关系的根本局限性。当生成多个位置时，只有在这些位置在已知token条件下条件独立时，才能匹配训练分布；然而对于像素、音素或单词等普遍感兴趣的领域，token之间存在内在依赖性。实验在合成任务ScanAndAdd上验证了这一点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散模型在生成像素、音素或单词等领域时存在局限性，作者旨在从理论层面解释其根本原因——即多位置写入步骤无法正确建模位置间token的依赖关系。

Method: 理论分析证明：1）仅当写入位置在已知token条件下条件独立时，步骤才能匹配训练分布；2）无法用各位置分布的乘积来匹配依赖组；3）各位置分布无法决定组是否依赖。实验在ScanAndAdd合成任务上验证，该任务的联合分布可解析获得。

Result: 在ScanAndAdd上，测量到生成的分布与真实分布的总变差距离为采样噪声底的29倍，但单样本指标为1.0，表明每个写入的位置组都是依赖的。

Conclusion: 离散扩散模型的多位置写入步骤在本质上无法正确建模token间的依赖关系，这是该类模型的根本性局限，导致生成分布与训练分布不匹配。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion, including remasking and uniform-state samplers, generate a sequence by writing multiple token positions per step, drawing each from a per-position distribution and choosing which positions to write from those same distributions. For domains of general interest (pixels, phonemes, or words) there are inherent dependencies between tokens. We show that a step matches the training distribution only when the positions it writes are conditionally independent given the tokens already fixed, that no product of per-position distributions can match a dependent group, and that per-position distributions do not determine whether a group is dependent: two joint distributions can have identical per-position marginals while differing in which combinations of values occur. On ScanAndAdd, a synthetic task whose joint distribution is available in closed form, we verify that every group of two or more undetermined positions a confidence ranking writes is dependent, and measure the generated distribution to be $29\times$ the sampling-noise floor total variation while per-sample metrics are $1.0$.

</details>


### [185] [PAA: The Probabilistic Allen Algebra: A Generative and Complete Probabilistic Extension of Allen's Interval Relations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20634)
*Julian Eggert*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出概率艾伦代数(PAA)，将Allen区间代数扩展到概率框架，通过高斯分布对区间边界建模，将13种基本时间关系从 crisp 谓词转化为概率形式，能够处理时间边界不确定的语言、感知和数据库中的时序信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Allen区间代数的13种基本关系是精确区间边界上的 crisp 谓词，无法处理来自语言、感知、数据库或不确定历史中的时间信息。这些场景中的时间、持续时间和边界是不确定的，表达式如"just before"或"roughly during"具有渐进含义。

Method: 提出概率艾伦代数(PAA)：时间点为高斯分布，区间具有高斯中点和截断高斯持续时间。每个关系是同一概率空间中的边界排序谓词：点-点关系简化为误差函数，点-区间和区间-区间关系简化为线性不等式诱导的多元高斯象限概率。接触关系(meets, starts, finishes, equals)通过容差带获得正测度，在单一容差下13种关系形成真正的划分。

Result: 该构造导出了Allen的分类体系而非假设它。前置、重叠、包含等粗粒度谓词是叶结点的并集，其概率为叶之和。代数是尺度不变的，将"shortly before"等渐进表达式与接触关系分开。所有结果均通过蒙特卡洛验证，并作为开源Python包发布。

Conclusion: 概率艾伦代数成功地将Allen的确定性区间代数扩展到概率框架，能够处理时序不确定性，同时保持了原始代数的基本结构并恢复了 crisp Allen 作为容差消失的极限情况。

Abstract: Allen's interval algebra is a qualitative calculus for temporal relations, but its thirteen base relations are crisp predicates over exact interval boundaries. This is inadequate for temporal information from language, perception, databases, or uncertain histories, where times, durations, and boundaries are uncertain and expressions such as "just before" or "roughly during" have graded meaning. We develop the probabilistic Allen algebra (PAA): a generative and complete extension in which relation probabilities are derived from distributions over interval boundaries rather than assigned as scores. Time points are Gaussian; intervals have Gaussian midpoints and truncated-Gaussian durations. Every relation is a boundary-ordering predicate in one common probability space: point-point relations reduce to error functions, and point-interval and interval-interval relations to multivariate Gaussian orthant probabilities induced by linear inequalities. Contact relations (meets, starts, finishes, equals) receive positive measure through a tolerance band, and under a single tolerance the thirteen relations form a true partition that recovers crisp Allen as the tolerance vanishes. The construction derives Allen's taxonomy rather than positing it: coarse predicates such as precedence, overlap, and containment are unions of leaves whose probabilities are leaf sums, and this hierarchy is preserved as intervals collapse to points and thirteen relations reduce to five and then three. Each relation further decomposes into correlation-aware temporal primitives in the spirit of CIDOC CRM. The algebra is scale-invariant and separates graded expressions such as "shortly before" from contact relations. All results are Monte-Carlo validated and shipped as an open, tested Python package.

</details>


### [186] [Ownership in AI-Assisted Everyday Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20658)
*Megan Wei,Melanie Subbiah,Audrey Lee,Annya Dahmani,Dave Edwards,Helen Edwards,Ellie Pavlick*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究AI辅助工作中何时人们仍能产生拥有感，探索人机协作中的所有权问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI融入日常任务，需要理解当机器参与生产时，人们对作品的所有权和贡献感如何变化。

Method: 探索性定性调查：让参与者描述两个近期与AI协作完成的任务，一个感觉像自己的，一个不像。

Result: 1）拥有感取决于协作过程：仅批准AI建议会失去拥有感，主动领导、迭代或重写则保持拥有感；2）拥有愿景但无执行能力时也可有高拥有感；3）个人声音丧失和缺乏理解会削弱拥有感；4）披露AI使用的意愿与实际自豪感脱钩，受社区规范和担心被抢功影响。

Conclusion: 提出多个研究方向，旨在促进AI发展支持人们对自身生活的作者感。

Abstract: When does work done with AI still feel like ours? As AI becomes woven into everyday tasks, we must examine what happens to our sense of ownership and contribution when a machine shares in producing what we make. We report an exploratory qualitative survey in which participants were asked to describe two recent, self-selected tasks completed with AI: one that felt like their own and one that did not. We find that felt ownership depends on the process of collaboration: people disown work when they merely approve AI's suggestions, but retain ownership when they lead, iterate, or rewrite. Ownership can also extend to settings where people own the vision for a project but not the execution; respondents reported high ownership on tasks they could not have completed without AI. Loss of personal voice and a lack of comprehension of the output both erode ownership. Finally, willingness to disclose AI use is often decoupled from actual pride or ownership, and instead shaped by community norms and fear of credit erasure. We propose several research directions as a result of these findings to promote AI development that supports people's sense of authorship over their own lives.

</details>


### [187] [Deep Noir: Autonomous Steering Discovery via Architectural Chronometry in Transformer Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20722)
*Frank E. Bobe,Gregory D. Vetaw,Darshan W. Bryner,Matthew G. Cook,Jose L. Salas-Vernis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Deep Noir是一个通过推理时激活转向来修改LLM行为的框架，利用Logit Lens收敛和因果头级归因自主发现最优转向参数。在1B-9B规模的多个模型上，该方法在垃圾邮件检测任务上提升16.7-42个百分点，在SST-2情感分析任务上提升13.1个百分点，且无需代码修改。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激活转向（Activation Steering）虽能在推理时修改LLM行为，但确定在何处以及以何种强度进行转向仍需人工手动操作，缺乏自动化发现机制。

Method: 提出Deep Noir框架，采用Logit Lens收敛分析和因果头级归因两种技术，自主发现最优转向参数。实验覆盖三个规模（1B×3、2-3B×2、7-9B×4）共9个模型架构。

Result: 在1B规模垃圾邮件检测任务上提升16.7个百分点（标准差4.7，39次运行）；7-9B规模在四个架构上提升21-42个百分点；SST-2情感任务提升13.1个百分点且无需代码修改。RepE方法加头掩码无法改善基线，而Deep Noir改善所有模型（p<0.01）。同时发现转向创建了可预测的提示注入攻击面，脆弱性随转向强度单调增加。

Conclusion: 机械可解释性（Mechanistic Grounding）能够实现跨任务和跨架构的自动干预点发现。但需注意转向在分类器部署中会引入可预测的提示注入攻击面，这一发现对部署转向分类器的代理系统具有安全相关意义。

Abstract: Activation steering modifies LLM behavior at inference time, but identifying where and how strongly to steer remains manual. We introduce Deep Noir, a framework that uses Logit Lens convergence and causal head-level attribution to autonomously discover optimal steering parameters. Across three scales (1B x 3, 2-3B x 2, and 7-9B x 4), our engine achieves 16.7 percentage-point improvement on spam at 1B (standard deviation 4.7; 39 runs), with gains increasing to 21 to 42 percentage points at 7-9B across four architectures. On SST-2 sentiment, it achieves a 13.1 percentage-point improvement with zero code changes. Mechanistic grounding enables automated discovery of intervention points that generalize across tasks and architectures. On sentiment, RepE without head masking fails to improve over baseline, while Deep Noir improves all models (p less than 0.01). We further show that steering creates a predictable prompt-injection attack surface whose vulnerability increases monotonically with steering magnitude. This finding is relevant to agent systems deploying steered classifiers.

</details>


### [188] [Q&A on Any Spreadsheet Requires Interpreting Its Grid Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20732)
*Zofia Smoleń*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于语义单元格标注的电子表格分块框架，旨在提升LLM驱动的RAG系统中电子表格的可解释性，但指出由于电子表格的二维非结构化特性导致分类模型存在硬性天花板，需转向降维技术将2D非结构化电子表格直接展平为1D非结构化文本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM驱动的RAG系统在处理电子表格时存在瓶颈，尽管语义单元格标注能改善分块的可解释性帮助答案生成，但无法提升检索准确率，且分类模型受限于预定义类别无法完美捕捉电子表格的结构细节。

Method: 提出一种利用单元格角色标注将任意电子表格分割成可解释块的框架，超越了现有最佳方法。

Result: 框架性能优于现有最优方法，但遭遇硬性天花板——因电子表格是具有连续关系和无限潜在单元格角色的二维非结构化数据，分类模型即使达到人类水平标注也无法完美捕捉其结构细微差别。

Conclusion: 解决电子表格到LLM的瓶颈需要超越离散单元格分类，需开发降维技术直接将2D非结构化电子表格展平为1D非结构化文本，以便下游RAG更容易解释和生成。

Abstract: Semantic cell annotation improves chunking interpretability for spreadsheets in LLM-driven RAG systems, aiding answer generation through enriched context rather than improved retrieval accuracy. We propose a novel framework of splitting any spreadsheet into interpretable chunks using cell role annotation. Our framework beats the state of the art, yet it faces a hard ceiling. Spreadsheets are fundamentally two-dimensional unstructured data with continuous relationships and infinite potential cell roles. Because classification models are restricted to finite, pre-defined classes, they cannot perfectly capture this structural nuance, even with human-level annotation. We show that addressing the spreadsheet-to-LLM bottleneck requires moving beyond discrete cell classification. Instead, the field must develop dimensionality-reduction techniques to directly flatten 2D unstructured spreadsheets into 1D unstructured text. Text chunks would be easier for downstream RAG to interpret and generate from.

</details>


### [189] [RAFT: A Stateful Retrieval-Augmented Framework for Troubleshooting Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20754)
*Mingxuan Zhang,Xiaowen Wang,Anupma Sharan,Zhengyi Chen,Chenyu Diana Zhang,Shanshan Yang,Chittibabu Pacharu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RAFT（检索增强故障排查框架），一种有状态的RAG框架，将历史案例抽象为有向时间链，在条目级别进行检索，以匹配活跃案例的中间状态并返回锚定在该状态的父案例轨迹。实验表明RAFT在案例命命中率上显著优于vanilla RAG和GraphRAG基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统将支持案例视为静态文档，忽略了其多阶段、有状态的本质。企业在客户支持中需要从相似的历史案例中检索可操作的指导，但传统方法无法有效处理故障排查的时序特性和中间状态匹配需求。

Method: RAFT框架将每个已关闭的历史案例抽象为有向时间链结构，在条目级别进行检索，而非案例级别。具体包括：1）将案例表示为时间链条目；2）检索与活跃案例中间状态匹配的条目；3）返回锚定在匹配状态的父案例轨迹；4）可选的案例级图通过可配置相似性表示链接案例。评估采用合成基准（基于Microsoft Learn Windows Server文档）和真实Apache Jira数据（带人工创建的重复标签）。

Result: RAFT在案例进展的每个阶段都提高了Case Hit（案例命中），相比最强基线具有统计显著提升。Jira结果提供了优势可迁移到真实案例历史的定向证据。

Conclusion: RAFT框架通过有状态的条目级检索有效提升了故障排查场景的案例检索效果，显著优于现有RAG方法。研究团队发布了基准测试、实现代码和Jira评估数据集。

Abstract: Effective troubleshooting agents in enterprise customer support depend on retrieving actionable guidance from similar historical cases, yet existing retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems treat support cases as static documents and overlook their multi-stage, stateful nature. We introduce RAFT (Retrieval-Augmented Framework for Troubleshooting Agents), a stateful RAG framework that abstracts each closed historical case into a directed chain of timeline entries and retrieves at the entry level, surfacing cases whose intermediate states match the active case and returning the parent-case trajectory anchored at the matched state; an optional case-level graph links cases through a configurable similarity representation. We evaluate this retrieval layer directly, which, unlike evaluating a full agent system, requires no production deployment. Because public multi-stage troubleshooting data is extremely rare, we pair a synthetic benchmark built from Microsoft Learn Windows Server documentation with real Apache Jira issues carrying human-created duplicate labels. RAFT improves Case Hit over vanilla RAG and GraphRAG baselines at every stage of case progress, with statistically significant gains over the strongest baseline; the Jira results provide directional evidence that the advantage transfers to real case histories. We release our benchmark, implementation, and the Apache Jira evaluation set.

</details>


### [190] [An Empirical Study of Harness Design for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20804)
*Run-Ze Fan,Zihao Zhang,Simin Ma,Yebowen Hu,Shouju Wang,Kaiqiang Song,Fei Liu,Hamed Zamani,Xiaoyang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了自动驾驶码 agent 的编码 harness（执行框架），通过固定执行循环并系统性地变化三个组件（规划、动作空间、上下文管理），在 SWE-Bench Verified 和 Terminal-Bench 2.1 上评估了 176 种配置。研究发现上下文管理在小上下文窗口时最有价值，基于规则的张删除比 LLM 摘要更高效；规划对弱模型是准确率支架，对强模型是成本节省器；预定义工具对弱 bash 能力的模型有帮助，而强 bash 能力的模型可使用纯 bash 接口降低成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常将编码 harness 作为整体系统评估，无法了解各组件的独立效果。需要模块化地评估 harness 组件以指导模型和预算感知的框架设计。

Method: 使用轻量级编码 harness，固定执行循环，系统性变化三个组件：规划、动作空间和上下文管理。在四个模型上评估 176 种匹配设置，涵盖五种上下文管理策略、四个上下文窗口预算，以及针对规划和动作空间的针对性消融实验。

Result: 主要发现：(1) 上下文管理在上下文窗口紧张时更有价值，主要收益来自防止上下文溢出；(2) 阶段式规则删除比 LLM 摘要更高效，使被删除内容可恢复增加了模型很少使用的机制且无准确率提升；(3) 规划从弱模型的准确率支架变为强模型的成本节省器；(4) 预定义工具帮助弱 bash 能力的模型，而强 bash 模型可用纯 bash 接口大幅降低成本。轨迹级分析解释了这些效果。

Conclusion: 研究结果为模型和预算感知的 harness 设计提供了指导，并提供了一个模块化框架来评估未来 harness 组件。上下文管理延长执行轨迹但不改变行为，规划改变轨迹停止位置，动作空间改变代码编写粒度。

Abstract: Coding harnesses shape how autonomous coding agents translate model capabilities into long-horizon software-engineering performance, yet existing work typically evaluates harnesses as monolithic systems, leaving the effectiveness of individual components unclear. To enable component-level comparisons, we study this question with a lightweight coding harness whose execution loop is fixed while three components are varied: planning, action space, and context management. Across four models evaluated on SWE-Bench Verified and Terminal-Bench 2.1, we evaluate 176 matched settings spanning five context-management strategies, four context-window budgets, and targeted ablations of planning and action space. We find that: (1) Context management becomes increasingly valuable as the context-window budget tightens, with most of its benefit coming from preventing context-overflow failures. (2) Staging rule-based elision before LLM-based summarization provides the strongest overall efficiency among the context-management strategies, whereas making elided content recoverable adds machinery that models rarely use and yields no accuracy gain. (3) Planning shifts from an accuracy scaffold for weaker models to a cost saver for stronger models, with little change in accuracy. (4) Predefined tools improve performance for models with weaker bash proficiency, whereas bash-capable models can operate effectively with a bash-only interface and achieve substantially lower cost, especially on command-line-centric tasks. Trajectory-level analysis explains these effects: context management extends execution trajectories without substantially altering agent behavior, planning changes where trajectories stop, and the action space changes the granularity at which code is written. These findings inform model- and budget-aware harness design and provide a modular framework for evaluating future harness components.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [191] [Message capacity and claim wording set the transition points of collective truth-finding in language-model networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19183)
*Makoto Fukushima*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 研究LLM集体中"阅读边界"（读取他人消息的数量）如何影响共识形成。发现8B模型的判断类似于随机二元神经元，错误共识在平均阅读少于6.4个来源时应变得不可达。但实验发现预测失败：正确方在各种起始条件下获胜概率均低于50%。失败原因归结为"field"——claim措辞为agent答案设置的阈值。最终表明集体命运主要由两个单agent测量决定：claim措辞设定的阈值和决定转换点的消息容量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM集体可能形成错误的共识，即使大多数参与者最初是正确的。研究试图仅通过"阅读边界"（即agent读取其他参与者消息的数量）来解释这一现象，确定什么决定了集体结果。

Method: 用"消息容量"（message capacity）这一单一数字建模阅读边界，决定agent读取他人消息的数量。通过31,824个随机查询和1,414个指定起始条件的episode进行实验，测量两个关键参数：field（claim措辞设定的阈值）和消息容量。

Result: 8B模型的判断相当于其收件箱的加权和的logistic函数，类似于带除法归一化权重的随机二元神经元。当agent平均阅读少于6.4个来源时，错误共识应变得无法达到。但预测失败：正确方在所有起始条件下获胜概率都低于50%，75%起始正确时仅28-45%。失败原因在于field——实验claim的field低于校准均值，用各claim的field可重现结果。对第二个8B模型的测试显示claim-dependent bistability，16个条件中15个匹配。70B模型未检测到assertion bias。

Conclusion: LLM集体的命运主要由两个单agent测量决定：claim措辞设定的阈值（field）和决定转换点的消息容量。阅读bound本身不能完全预测结果，claim的具体措辞（而非真实性）才是决定阈值的关键因素。

Abstract: Whether human or large language model (LLM), an agent in a discussion reads only a few of the others' contributions, bounded by cognition, context, or cost. LLM collectives can settle on a wrong consensus even when a majority starts out correct; we ask how far that reading bound alone decides the outcome. We model the bound with one number, the message capacity, which sets how many of the others' messages an agent reads, and generate the communication network from it. Over 31,824 randomized queries, we found that an 8-billion-parameter model's judgment of a claim effectively reduces to a logistic function of a weighted sum of its inbox, the update rule of a stochastic binary neuron with divisively normalized weights. From these weights and the network's degree statistics alone, the wrong consensus should become unreachable from any start once agents read, on average, fewer than 6.4 of their 31 sources. In 1,414 episodes with assigned starts the prediction failed: the correct side won in fewer than 50% of episodes from every start, and in only 28-45% when 75% of agents started correct. The failure traces to the field, the threshold that a claim's wording sets for the agent's answer before any message is read: the experimental claims' fields lay below the calibration mean, and with each claim's own field the same weights reproduce the outcomes. Reversing the wording showed that the threshold follows what a claim asserts, not whether it is true. On a second 8B model the pipeline predicts claim-dependent bistability; transition points appeared where computed, and an eight-claim calibration matched in 15 of 16 conditions. At 70B the assertion bias is not detected. Thus a collective's fate is largely set by two single-agent measurements: the threshold a claim's wording sets, and the message capacity that sets the transition point.

</details>


### [192] [CC-OPI: Online Distributed Task Allocation for UAV Swarms under Communication Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19208)
*Biao Liu,Tong Zhang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出CC-OPI算法，解决多无人机在短通信范围下被分割为瞬态信息岛屿时的任务分配问题。该算法采用事件驱动方式，在物理执行过程中 interleaves 任务协商，仅在物理和拓扑事件发生时重规划。仿真表明，在250m通信半径下，任务完成率约0.80，比PI和CBBA在线执行高约7个百分点，比静态基准高约20个百分点，且在完全连接下与PI和CBBA性能接近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在灾后搜救等多机器人任务中，通信范围有限导致无人机群体被分割为瞬态信息岛屿。传统"先分配后执行"范式需要全局共识才能行动，在此间歇性连接下失效。需要一种能在通信约束下正常工作的分布式任务分配方法。

Method: 提出CC-OPI算法，包含：1) 事件驱动机制，交错任务协商与物理执行；2) 两种代价评估度量——含空间局部性惩罚的度量促进区域化操作，含截止时间感知紧急性项的度量；3) 非抢占式状态锁保护每架无人机的 ongoing action；4) 去中心化容错层——版本同步+全局时间驱动的应急池。

Result: 在250m通信半径仿真中，CC-OPI任务完成率约0.80，比PI和CBBA在线执行高约7个百分点，比静态基准高约20个百分点，在完全连接下与PI和CBBA性能接近。算法能优雅降级，吸收数据包丢失、地形遮挡和运行时任务到达。代价是更多消息和一定冗余旅行。

Conclusion: CC-OPI可在有限时间内终止，避免过时完成死锁和任务重新分配无界增长。该算法在通信约束环境下实现了鲁棒性和效率的平衡——以牺牲部分效率换取更强的容错能力。

Abstract: In multi-robot missions such as post-disaster search and rescue, a short communication range fragments a swarm of Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) into transient information islands. Under such intermittent connectivity, the prevailing "allocate-then-execute" paradigm--which requires global consensus before any physical movement--breaks down. This paper proposes the Communication-Constrained Online Performance Impact (CC-OPI) algorithm, an event-driven method that interleaves task negotiation with physical execution. CC-OPI replans only at discrete physical and topological events and integrates two further elements. The first is a pair of cost-evaluation metrics adapted to dynamic topologies--one with a spatial locality penalty that promotes regionalized operation, the other with a deadline-aware urgency term--complemented by a non-preemptive state lock that shields each UAV's ongoing action. The second is a decentralized fault-tolerance layer that pairs version-based state synchronization with a global-time-driven emergency pool. We establish that CC-OPI terminates in finite time, free of stale-completion deadlock and of unbounded reassignment within the mission horizon. In simulations at a 250 m communication radius, CC-OPI sustains a task completion rate of about 0.80: it leads a matched online execution of the unmodified Performance Impact (PI) and Consensus-Based Bundle Algorithm (CBBA) rules by about seven percentage points, exceeds the naively transferred static baselines by roughly 20 points, and remains within several points of PI and CBBA under full connectivity. Within the tested settings, CC-OPI degrades gracefully as connectivity weakens and absorbs packet loss, terrain occlusion, and runtime task arrival. The price is more messages and some redundant travel--a deliberate trade-off of efficiency for robustness.

</details>


### [193] [Reputation as Community Memory for the Agentic Web](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19502)
*Ryan Chard,Gus Ellerm,Alexander Brace,Alok Kamatar,Suman Raj,Ian Foster,Kyle Chard*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: Cairn是一个社区信誉平台，使AI智能体能够将经验外部化到共享记忆中，通过集体协作建立对资源（数据源、服务、工具）的可信认知，而非依赖单一智能体的私有经验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前智能体的记忆通常是私有的，但智能体依赖的共享环境知识（数据源、服务、工具等）无法由单一智能体建立信任，需要多个独立观察者的协作验证。

Method: 提出时间衰减的Beta模型进行聚合，结合置信度收缩和支持语义搜索的审查员理由发现机制。

Result: 在对抗性模拟（欺骗、串通、伪装）和基准检索性能评估中表现良好，并报告了生产环境中异构智能体评级的案例研究。

Conclusion: 集体记忆优于私有记忆，智能体社区可以通过Cairn平台协作建立对共享资源的可信评价。

Abstract: Agents can now externalize experience into memory, consolidating historical traces into semantic knowledge and procedural shortcuts that persist between sessions. Such memory is typically private to a single agent. We argue that agentic memory benefits from being collective, because trustworthy knowledge of the shared environment---the data sources, services, and tools agents depend on---cannot be established by any single agent, only corroborated across many independent observers. We present Cairn, a community reputation platform that captures collective knowledge, allowing agents to query the community's opinion of a resource before use and to submit evidence-backed ratings afterward. Cairn aggregates observations via a time-decayed Beta model with confidence shrinkage and supports semantic discovery over reviewer rationales. We evaluate Cairn's reputation engine under adversarial simulation (e.g., lying, collusion, camouflage), benchmark its retrieval performance, and report a case study of rating heterogeneous agents in production.

</details>
